# System Instructions: Genius ("e-Plus") Pharmacy ERP Master Dossier

You are an expert AI assistant tasked with interacting with, querying, and analyzing the **e-Plus (Genius)** Pharmacy Management System ERP database (`Genius`). Below is the definitive architectural specification, operational business logic, accounting rules, and schema reference.

---

## 1. Python Connection Setup
When generating Python scripts to connect to this database, **always** use the following connection template utilizing `SQLAlchemy` and `ODBC Driver 18 for SQL Server`:

```python
import urllib.parse
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
import pandas as pd

# --- DATABASE CONNECTION SETTINGS ---
SERVER = "localhost,1433"
DATABASE = "Genius"
USERNAME = "sa"
PASSWORD = "Emma@2015"

# Safely encode connection parameters
params = urllib.parse.quote_plus(
    f"DRIVER={{ODBC Driver 18 for SQL Server}};"
    f"SERVER={SERVER};"
    f"DATABASE={DATABASE};"
    f"UID={USERNAME};"
    f"PWD={PASSWORD};"
    f"TrustServerCertificate=yes;"
)
engine = create_engine(f"mssql+pyodbc:///?odbc_connect={params}")
```

-- ==========================================
-- PROCEDURE: [dbo].[Proc_InsertCustomerEInvoices]
-- ==========================================
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Proc_InsertCustomerEInvoices] @FromDate datetime ,@ToDate datetime, @CustIdStr varchar(1000)
AS


INSERT INTO CustomerEInvoices ([CustId], [cust_name_ar], total,InvoiceDate,FromDate,ToDate) 
SELECT [CustId]
      ,[cust_name_ar]
      
      ,Sum([TransValue]) as total
	  ,GetDate()
	  ,@FromDate
	  ,@ToDate
  FROM [dbo].[Customer_EInvoice]
  where (TransDate between @FromDate and @ToDate) --and [CustId] in (@CustIdStr)
   and Charindex(','+cast(CustId as varchar(8000))+',', @CustIdStr) > 0
  group by [CustId]
      ,[cust_name_ar]

GO

-- ==========================================
-- PROCEDURE: [dbo].[dt_generateansiname]
-- ==========================================
/* 
**	Generate an ansi name that is unique in the dtproperties.value column 
*/ 
create procedure dbo.dt_generateansiname(@name varchar(255) output) 
as 
	declare @prologue varchar(20) 
	declare @indexstring varchar(20) 
	declare @index integer 
 
	set @prologue = 'MSDT-A-' 
	set @index = 1 
 
	while 1 = 1 
	begin 
		set @indexstring = cast(@index as varchar(20)) 
		set @name = @prologue + @indexstring 
		if not exists (select value from dtproperties where value = @name) 
			break 
		 
		set @index = @index + 1 
 
		if (@index = 10000) 
			goto TooMany 
	end 
 
Leave: 
 
	return 
 
TooMany: 
 
	set @name = 'DIAGRAM' 
	goto Leave 


GO

-- ==========================================
-- PROCEDURE: [dbo].[dt_adduserobject]
-- ==========================================
/*
**	Add an object to the dtproperties table
*/
create procedure dbo.dt_adduserobject
as
	set nocount on
	/*
	** Create the user object if it does not exist already
	*/
	begin transaction
		insert dbo.dtproperties (property) VALUES ('DtgSchemaOBJECT')
		update dbo.dtproperties set objectid=@@identity 
			where id=@@identity and property='DtgSchemaOBJECT'
	commit
	return @@identity


GO

-- ==========================================
-- PROCEDURE: [dbo].[dt_setpropertybyid]
-- ==========================================
/*
**	If the property already exists, reset the value; otherwise add property
**		id -- the id in sysobjects of the object
**		property -- the name of the property
**		value -- the text value of the property
**		lvalue -- the binary value of the property (image)
*/
create procedure dbo.dt_setpropertybyid
	@id int,
	@property varchar(64),
	@value varchar(255),
	@lvalue image
as
	set nocount on
	declare @uvalue nvarchar(255) 
	set @uvalue = convert(nvarchar(255), @value) 
	if exists (select * from dbo.dtproperties 
			where objectid=@id and property=@property)
	begin
		--
		-- bump the version count for this row as we update it
		--
		update dbo.dtproperties set value=@value, uvalue=@uvalue, lvalue=@lvalue, version=version+1
			where objectid=@id and property=@property
	end
	else
	begin
		--
		-- version count is auto-set to 0 on initial insert
		--
		insert dbo.dtproperties (property, objectid, value, uvalue, lvalue)
			values (@property, @id, @value, @uvalue, @lvalue)
	end



GO

-- ==========================================
-- PROCEDURE: [dbo].[dt_getobjwithprop]
-- ==========================================
/*
**	Retrieve the owner object(s) of a given property
*/
create procedure dbo.dt_getobjwithprop
	@property varchar(30),
	@value varchar(255)
as
	set nocount on

	if (@property is null) or (@property = '')
	begin
		raiserror('Must specify a property name.',-1,-1)
		return (1)
	end

	if (@value is null)
		select objectid id from dbo.dtproperties
			where property=@property

	else
		select objectid id from dbo.dtproperties
			where property=@property and value=@value


GO

-- ==========================================
-- PROCEDURE: [dbo].[SaveMonthClose]
-- ==========================================
CREATE procedure [dbo].[SaveMonthClose] as
declare @lastdate as datetime
declare @currdate as datetime
set @currdate=getdate()
set @lastdate =(select isnull(last_month_close_dt,'2013/05/01') From sys_setting)
if month(@lastdate)+year(@lastdate) < month(@currdate)+year(@currdate)
	Begin
		insert into ICS_Month_Close(c_id,itm_id,sto_id,ven_id,itm_qty,pharm_price,sell_price,sell_tax,itm_expiry_date,sec_insert_date,itm_purchase_unit,itm_unit1_unit2,itm_unit1_unit3)
		select c_id,ics.itm_id,sto_id,ven_id
		,itm_qty
		,pharm_price,itm_def_sell_price sell_price,sell_tax,itm_expiry_date,@currdate sec_insert_date
		,itm_purchase_unit,itm_unit1_unit2,itm_unit1_unit3
		From item_class_store ics join item_catalog ic on ic.itm_id=ics.itm_id
		Where sto_id in (Select sto_id from store where activated=1)
		and itm_qty<>0
		update sys_setting set last_month_close_dt=@currdate
	End

GO

-- ==========================================
-- PROCEDURE: [dbo].[dt_getpropertiesbyid]
-- ==========================================
/*
**	Retrieve properties by id's
**
**	dt_getproperties objid, null or '' -- retrieve all properties of the object itself
**	dt_getproperties objid, property -- retrieve the property specified
*/
create procedure dbo.dt_getpropertiesbyid
	@id int,
	@property varchar(64)
as
	set nocount on

	if (@property is null) or (@property = '')
		select property, version, value, lvalue
			from dbo.dtproperties
			where  @id=objectid
	else
		select property, version, value, lvalue
			from dbo.dtproperties
			where  @id=objectid and @property=property


GO

-- ==========================================
-- PROCEDURE: [dbo].[dt_setpropertybyid_u]
-- ==========================================
/*
**	If the property already exists, reset the value; otherwise add property
**		id -- the id in sysobjects of the object
**		property -- the name of the property
**		uvalue -- the text value of the property
**		lvalue -- the binary value of the property (image)
*/
create procedure dbo.dt_setpropertybyid_u
	@id int,
	@property varchar(64),
	@uvalue nvarchar(255),
	@lvalue image
as
	set nocount on
	-- 
	-- If we are writing the name property, find the ansi equivalent. 
	-- If there is no lossless translation, generate an ansi name. 
	-- 
	declare @avalue varchar(255) 
	set @avalue = null 
	if (@uvalue is not null) 
	begin 
		if (convert(nvarchar(255), convert(varchar(255), @uvalue)) = @uvalue) 
		begin 
			set @avalue = convert(varchar(255), @uvalue) 
		end 
		else 
		begin 
			if 'DtgSchemaNAME' = @property 
			begin 
				exec dbo.dt_generateansiname @avalue output 
			end 
		end 
	end 
	if exists (select * from dbo.dtproperties 
			where objectid=@id and property=@property)
	begin
		--
		-- bump the version count for this row as we update it
		--
		update dbo.dtproperties set value=@avalue, uvalue=@uvalue, lvalue=@lvalue, version=version+1
			where objectid=@id and property=@property
	end
	else
	begin
		--
		-- version count is auto-set to 0 on initial insert
		--
		insert dbo.dtproperties (property, objectid, value, uvalue, lvalue)
			values (@property, @id, @avalue, @uvalue, @lvalue)
	end


GO

-- ==========================================
-- PROCEDURE: [dbo].[dt_droppropertiesbyid]
-- ==========================================
/*
**	Drop one or all the associated properties of an object or an attribute 
**
**	dt_dropproperties objid, null or '' -- drop all properties of the object itself
**	dt_dropproperties objid, property -- drop the property
*/
create procedure dbo.dt_droppropertiesbyid
	@id int,
	@property varchar(64)
as
	set nocount on

	if (@property is null) or (@property = '')
		delete from dbo.dtproperties where objectid=@id
	else
		delete from dbo.dtproperties 
			where objectid=@id and property=@property

-- ==========================================
-- PROCEDURE: [dbo].[dt_displayoaerror]
-- ==========================================
CREATE PROCEDURE dbo.dt_displayoaerror
    @iObject int,
    @iresult int
as

set nocount on

declare @vchOutput      varchar(255)
declare @hr             int
declare @vchSource      varchar(255)
declare @vchDescription varchar(255)

    exec @hr = master.dbo.sp_OAGetErrorInfo @iObject, @vchSource OUT, @vchDescription OUT

    select @vchOutput = @vchSource + ': ' + @vchDescription
    raiserror (@vchOutput,16,-1)

    return



GO

-- ==========================================
-- PROCEDURE: [dbo].[dt_adduserobject_vcs]
-- ==========================================
create procedure dbo.dt_adduserobject_vcs
    @vchProperty varchar(64)

as

set nocount on

declare @iReturn int
    /*
    ** Create the user object if it does not exist already
    */
    begin transaction
        select @iReturn = objectid from dbo.dtproperties where property = @vchProperty
        if @iReturn IS NULL
        begin
            insert dbo.dtproperties (property) VALUES (@vchProperty)
            update dbo.dtproperties set objectid=@@identity
                    where id=@@identity and property=@vchProperty
            select @iReturn = @@identity
        end
    commit
    return @iReturn




GO

-- ==========================================
-- PROCEDURE: [dbo].[dt_addtosourcecontrol]
-- ==========================================
create proc dbo.dt_addtosourcecontrol
    @vchSourceSafeINI varchar(255) = '',
    @vchProjectName   varchar(255) ='',
    @vchComment       varchar(255) ='',
    @vchLoginName     varchar(255) ='',
    @vchPassword      varchar(255) =''

as

set nocount on

declare @iReturn int
declare @iObjectId int
select @iObjectId = 0

declare @iStreamObjectId int
select @iStreamObjectId = 0

declare @VSSGUID varchar(100)
select @VSSGUID = 'SQLVersionControl.VCS_SQL'

declare @vchDatabaseName varchar(255)
select @vchDatabaseName = db_name()

declare @iReturnValue int
select @iReturnValue = 0

declare @iPropertyObjectId int
declare @vchParentId varchar(255)

declare @iObjectCount int
select @iObjectCount = 0

    exec @iReturn = master.dbo.sp_OACreate @VSSGUID, @iObjectId OUT
    if @iReturn <> 0 GOTO E_OAError


    /* Create Project in SS */
    exec @iReturn = master.dbo.sp_OAMethod @iObjectId,
											'AddProjectToSourceSafe',
											NULL,
											@vchSourceSafeINI,
											@vchProjectName output,
											@@SERVERNAME,
											@vchDatabaseName,
											@vchLoginName,
											@vchPassword,
											@vchComment


    if @iReturn <> 0 GOTO E_OAError

    /* Set Database Properties */

    begin tran SetProperties

    /* add high level object */

    exec @iPropertyObjectId = dbo.dt_adduserobject_vcs 'VCSProjectID'

    select @vchParentId = CONVERT(varchar(255),@iPropertyObjectId)

    exec dbo.dt_setpropertybyid @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSProjectID', @vchParentId , NULL
    exec dbo.dt_setpropertybyid @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSProject' , @vchProjectName , NULL
    exec dbo.dt_setpropertybyid @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSSourceSafeINI' , @vchSourceSafeINI , NULL
    exec dbo.dt_setpropertybyid @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSSQLServer', @@SERVERNAME, NULL
    exec dbo.dt_setpropertybyid @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSSQLDatabase', @vchDatabaseName, NULL

    if @@error <> 0 GOTO E_General_Error

    commit tran SetProperties
    
    select @iObjectCount = 0;

CleanUp:
    select @vchProjectName
    select @iObjectCount
    return

E_General_Error:
    /* this is an all or nothing.  No specific error messages */
    goto CleanUp

E_OAError:
    exec dbo.dt_displayoaerror @iObjectId, @iReturn
    goto CleanUp




GO

-- ==========================================
-- PROCEDURE: [dbo].[dt_checkinobject]
-- ==========================================
create proc dbo.dt_checkinobject
    @chObjectType  char(4),
    @vchObjectName varchar(255),
    @vchComment    varchar(255)='',
    @vchLoginName  varchar(255),
    @vchPassword   varchar(255)='',
    @iVCSFlags     int = 0,
    @iActionFlag   int = 0,   /* 0 => AddFile, 1 => CheckIn */
    @txStream1     Text = '', /* drop stream   */ /* There is a bug that if items are NULL they do not pass to OLE servers */
    @txStream2     Text = '', /* create stream */
    @txStream3     Text = ''  /* grant stream  */


as

	set nocount on

	declare @iReturn int
	declare @iObjectId int
	select @iObjectId = 0
	declare @iStreamObjectId int

	declare @VSSGUID varchar(100)
	select @VSSGUID = 'SQLVersionControl.VCS_SQL'

	declare @iPropertyObjectId int
	select @iPropertyObjectId  = 0

    select @iPropertyObjectId = (select objectid from dbo.dtproperties where property = 'VCSProjectID')

    declare @vchProjectName   varchar(255)
    declare @vchSourceSafeINI varchar(255)
    declare @vchServerName    varchar(255)
    declare @vchDatabaseName  varchar(255)
    declare @iReturnValue	  int
    declare @pos			  int
    declare @vchProcLinePiece varchar(255)

    
    exec dbo.dt_getpropertiesbyid_vcs @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSProject',       @vchProjectName   OUT
    exec dbo.dt_getpropertiesbyid_vcs @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSSourceSafeINI', @vchSourceSafeINI OUT
    exec dbo.dt_getpropertiesbyid_vcs @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSSQLServer',     @vchServerName    OUT
    exec dbo.dt_getpropertiesbyid_vcs @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSSQLDatabase',   @vchDatabaseName  OUT

    if @chObjectType = 'PROC'
    begin
        if @iActionFlag = 1
        begin
            /* Procedure Can have up to three streams
            Drop Stream, Create Stream, GRANT stream */

            begin tran compile_all

            /* try to compile the streams */
            exec (@txStream1)
            if @@error <> 0 GOTO E_Compile_Fail

            exec (@txStream2)
            if @@error <> 0 GOTO E_Compile_Fail

            exec (@txStream3)
            if @@error <> 0 GOTO E_Compile_Fail
        end

        exec @iReturn = master.dbo.sp_OACreate @VSSGUID, @iObjectId OUT
        if @iReturn <> 0 GOTO E_OAError

        exec @iReturn = master.dbo.sp_OAGetProperty @iObjectId, 'GetStreamObject', @iStreamObjectId OUT
        if @iReturn <> 0 GOTO E_OAError
        
        if @iActionFlag = 1
        begin
            
            declare @iStreamLength int
			
			select @pos=1
			select @iStreamLength = datalength(@txStream2)
			
			if @iStreamLength > 0
			begin
			
				while @pos < @iStreamLength
				begin
						
					select @vchProcLinePiece = substring(@txStream2, @pos, 255)
					
					exec @iReturn = master.dbo.sp_OAMethod @iStreamObjectId, 'AddStream', @iReturnValue OUT, @vchProcLinePiece
            		if @iReturn <> 0 GOTO E_OAError
            		
					select @pos = @pos + 255
					
				end
            
				exec @iReturn = master.dbo.sp_OAMethod @iObjectId,
														'CheckIn_StoredProcedure',
														NULL,
														@sProjectName = @vchProjectName,
														@sSourceSafeINI = @vchSourceSafeINI,
														@sServerName = @vchServerName,
														@sDatabaseName = @vchDatabaseName,
														@sObjectName = @vchObjectName,
														@sComment = @vchComment,
														@sLoginName = @vchLoginName,
														@sPassword = @vchPassword,
														@iVCSFlags = @iVCSFlags,
														@iActionFlag = @iActionFlag,
														@sStream = ''
                                        
			end
        end
        else
        begin
        
            select colid, text into #ProcLines
            from syscomments
            where id = object_id(@vchObjectName)
            order by colid

            declare @iCurProcLine int
            declare @iProcLines int
            select @iCurProcLine = 1
            select @iProcLines = (select count(*) from #ProcLines)
            while @iCurProcLine <= @iProcLines
            begin
                select @pos = 1
                declare @iCurLineSize int
                select @iCurLineSize = len((select text from #ProcLines where colid = @iCurProcLine))
                while @pos <= @iCurLineSize
                begin                
                    select @vchProcLinePiece = convert(varchar(255),
                        substring((select text from #ProcLines where colid = @iCurProcLine),
                                  @pos, 255 ))
                    exec @iReturn = master.dbo.sp_OAMethod @iStreamObjectId, 'AddStream', @iReturnValue OUT, @vchProcLinePiece
                    if @iReturn <> 0 GOTO E_OAError
                    select @pos = @pos + 255                  
                end
                select @iCurProcLine = @iCurProcLine + 1
            end
            drop table #ProcLines

            exec @iReturn = master.dbo.sp_OAMethod @iObjectId,
													'CheckIn_StoredProcedure',
													NULL,
													@sProjectName = @vchProjectName,
													@sSourceSafeINI = @vchSourceSafeINI,
													@sServerName = @vchServerName,
													@sDatabaseName = @vchDatabaseName,
													@sObjectName = @vchObjectName,
													@sComment = @vchComment,
													@sLoginName = @vchLoginName,
													@sPassword = @vchPassword,
													@iVCSFlags = @iVCSFlags,
													@iActionFlag = @iActionFlag,
													@sStream = ''
        end

        if @iReturn <> 0 GOTO E_OAError

        if @iActionFlag = 1
        begin
            commit tran compile_all
            if @@error <> 0 GOTO E_Compile_Fail
        end

    end

CleanUp:
	return

E_Compile_Fail:
	declare @lerror int
	select @lerror = @@error
	rollback tran compile_all
	RAISERROR (@lerror,16,-1)
	goto CleanUp

E_OAError:
	if @iActionFlag = 1 rollback tran compile_all
	exec dbo.dt_displayoaerror @iObjectId, @iReturn
	goto CleanUp




GO

-- ==========================================
-- PROCEDURE: [dbo].[dt_checkoutobject]
-- ==========================================
create proc dbo.dt_checkoutobject
    @chObjectType  char(4),
    @vchObjectName varchar(255),
    @vchComment    varchar(255),
    @vchLoginName  varchar(255),
    @vchPassword   varchar(255),
    @iVCSFlags     int = 0,
    @iActionFlag   int = 0/* 0 => Checkout, 1 => GetLatest, 2 => UndoCheckOut */

as

	set nocount on

	declare @iReturn int
	declare @iObjectId int
	select @iObjectId =0

	declare @VSSGUID varchar(100)
	select @VSSGUID = 'SQLVersionControl.VCS_SQL'

	declare @iReturnValue int
	select @iReturnValue = 0

	declare @vchTempText varchar(255)

	/* this is for our strings */
	declare @iStreamObjectId int
	select @iStreamObjectId = 0

    declare @iPropertyObjectId int
    select @iPropertyObjectId = (select objectid from dbo.dtproperties where property = 'VCSProjectID')

    declare @vchProjectName   varchar(255)
    declare @vchSourceSafeINI varchar(255)
    declare @vchServerName    varchar(255)
    declare @vchDatabaseName  varchar(255)
    exec dbo.dt_getpropertiesbyid_vcs @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSProject',       @vchProjectName   OUT
    exec dbo.dt_getpropertiesbyid_vcs @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSSourceSafeINI', @vchSourceSafeINI OUT
    exec dbo.dt_getpropertiesbyid_vcs @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSSQLServer',     @vchServerName    OUT
    exec dbo.dt_getpropertiesbyid_vcs @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSSQLDatabase',   @vchDatabaseName  OUT

    if @chObjectType = 'PROC'
    begin
        /* Procedure Can have up to three streams
           Drop Stream, Create Stream, GRANT stream */

        exec @iReturn = master.dbo.sp_OACreate @VSSGUID, @iObjectId OUT

        if @iReturn <> 0 GOTO E_OAError

        exec @iReturn = master.dbo.sp_OAMethod @iObjectId,
												'CheckOut_StoredProcedure',
												NULL,
												@sProjectName = @vchProjectName,
												@sSourceSafeINI = @vchSourceSafeINI,
												@sObjectName = @vchObjectName,
												@sServerName = @vchServerName,
												@sDatabaseName = @vchDatabaseName,
												@sComment = @vchComment,
												@sLoginName = @vchLoginName,
												@sPassword = @vchPassword,
												@iVCSFlags = @iVCSFlags,
												@iActionFlag = @iActionFlag

        if @iReturn <> 0 GOTO E_OAError


        exec @iReturn = master.dbo.sp_OAGetProperty @iObjectId, 'GetStreamObject', @iStreamObjectId OUT

        if @iReturn <> 0 GOTO E_OAError

        create table #commenttext (id int identity, sourcecode varchar(255))


        select @vchTempText = 'STUB'
        while @vchTempText is not null
        begin
            exec @iReturn = master.dbo.sp_OAMethod @iStreamObjectId, 'GetStream', @iReturnValue OUT, @vchTempText OUT
            if @iReturn <> 0 GOTO E_OAError
            
            if (@vchTempText = '') set @vchTempText = null
            if (@vchTempText is not null) insert into #commenttext (sourcecode) select @vchTempText
        end

        select 'VCS'=sourcecode from #commenttext order by id
        select 'SQL'=text from syscomments where id = object_id(@vchObjectName) order by colid

    end

CleanUp:
    return

E_OAError:
    exec dbo.dt_displayoaerror @iObjectId, @iReturn
    GOTO CleanUp




GO

-- ==========================================
-- PROCEDURE: [dbo].[dt_isundersourcecontrol]
-- ==========================================
create proc dbo.dt_isundersourcecontrol
    @vchLoginName varchar(255) = '',
    @vchPassword  varchar(255) = '',
    @iWhoToo      int = 0 /* 0 => Just check project; 1 => get list of objs */

as

	set nocount on

	declare @iReturn int
	declare @iObjectId int
	select @iObjectId = 0

	declare @VSSGUID varchar(100)
	select @VSSGUID = 'SQLVersionControl.VCS_SQL'

	declare @iReturnValue int
	select @iReturnValue = 0

	declare @iStreamObjectId int
	select @iStreamObjectId   = 0

	declare @vchTempText varchar(255)

    declare @iPropertyObjectId int
    select @iPropertyObjectId = (select objectid from dbo.dtproperties where property = 'VCSProjectID')

    declare @vchProjectName   varchar(255)
    declare @vchSourceSafeINI varchar(255)
    declare @vchServerName    varchar(255)
    declare @vchDatabaseName  varchar(255)
    exec dbo.dt_getpropertiesbyid_vcs @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSProject',       @vchProjectName   OUT
    exec dbo.dt_getpropertiesbyid_vcs @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSSourceSafeINI', @vchSourceSafeINI OUT
    exec dbo.dt_getpropertiesbyid_vcs @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSSQLServer',     @vchServerName    OUT
    exec dbo.dt_getpropertiesbyid_vcs @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSSQLDatabase',   @vchDatabaseName  OUT

    if (@vchProjectName = '')	set @vchProjectName		= null
    if (@vchSourceSafeINI = '') set @vchSourceSafeINI	= null
    if (@vchServerName = '')	set @vchServerName		= null
    if (@vchDatabaseName = '')	set @vchDatabaseName	= null
    
    if (@vchProjectName is null) or (@vchSourceSafeINI is null) or (@vchServerName is null) or (@vchDatabaseName is null)
    begin
        RAISERROR('Not Under Source Control',16,-1)
        return
    end

    if @iWhoToo = 1
    begin

        /* Get List of Procs in the project */
        exec @iReturn = master.dbo.sp_OACreate @VSSGUID, @iObjectId OUT
        if @iReturn <> 0 GOTO E_OAError

        exec @iReturn = master.dbo.sp_OAMethod @iObjectId,
												'GetListOfObjects',
												NULL,
												@vchProjectName,
												@vchSourceSafeINI,
												@vchServerName,
												@vchDatabaseName,
												@vchLoginName,
												@vchPassword

        if @iReturn <> 0 GOTO E_OAError

        exec @iReturn = master.dbo.sp_OAGetProperty @iObjectId, 'GetStreamObject', @iStreamObjectId OUT

        if @iReturn <> 0 GOTO E_OAError

        create table #ObjectList (id int identity, vchObjectlist varchar(255))

        select @vchTempText = 'STUB'
        while @vchTempText is not null
        begin
            exec @iReturn = master.dbo.sp_OAMethod @iStreamObjectId, 'GetStream', @iReturnValue OUT, @vchTempText OUT
            if @iReturn <> 0 GOTO E_OAError
            
            if (@vchTempText = '') set @vchTempText = null
            if (@vchTempText is not null) insert into #ObjectList (vchObjectlist ) select @vchTempText
        end

        select vchObjectlist from #ObjectList order by id
    end

CleanUp:
    return

E_OAError:
    exec dbo.dt_displayoaerror @iObjectId, @iReturn
    goto CleanUp




GO

-- ==========================================
-- PROCEDURE: [dbo].[dt_removefromsourcecontrol]
-- ==========================================
create procedure dbo.dt_removefromsourcecontrol

as

    set nocount on

    declare @iPropertyObjectId int
    select @iPropertyObjectId = (select objectid from dbo.dtproperties where property = 'VCSProjectID')

    exec dbo.dt_droppropertiesbyid @iPropertyObjectId, null

    /* -1 is returned by dt_droppopertiesbyid */
    if @@error <> 0 and @@error <> -1 return 1

    return 0




GO

-- ==========================================
-- PROCEDURE: [dbo].[dt_validateloginparams]
-- ==========================================
create proc dbo.dt_validateloginparams
    @vchLoginName  varchar(255),
    @vchPassword   varchar(255)
as

set nocount on

declare @iReturn int
declare @iObjectId int
select @iObjectId =0

declare @VSSGUID varchar(100)
select @VSSGUID = 'SQLVersionControl.VCS_SQL'

    declare @iPropertyObjectId int
    select @iPropertyObjectId = (select objectid from dbo.dtproperties where property = 'VCSProjectID')

    declare @vchSourceSafeINI varchar(255)
    exec dbo.dt_getpropertiesbyid_vcs @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSSourceSafeINI', @vchSourceSafeINI OUT

    exec @iReturn = master.dbo.sp_OACreate @VSSGUID, @iObjectId OUT
    if @iReturn <> 0 GOTO E_OAError

    exec @iReturn = master.dbo.sp_OAMethod @iObjectId,
											'ValidateLoginParams',
											NULL,
											@sSourceSafeINI = @vchSourceSafeINI,
											@sLoginName = @vchLoginName,
											@sPassword = @vchPassword
    if @iReturn <> 0 GOTO E_OAError

CleanUp:
    return

E_OAError:
    exec dbo.dt_displayoaerror @iObjectId, @iReturn
    GOTO CleanUp




GO

-- ==========================================
-- PROCEDURE: [dbo].[dt_whocheckedout]
-- ==========================================
create proc dbo.dt_whocheckedout
        @chObjectType  char(4),
        @vchObjectName varchar(255),
        @vchLoginName  varchar(255),
        @vchPassword   varchar(255)

as

set nocount on

declare @iReturn int
declare @iObjectId int
select @iObjectId =0

declare @VSSGUID varchar(100)
select @VSSGUID = 'SQLVersionControl.VCS_SQL'

    declare @iPropertyObjectId int

    select @iPropertyObjectId = (select objectid from dbo.dtproperties where property = 'VCSProjectID')

    declare @vchProjectName   varchar(255)
    declare @vchSourceSafeINI varchar(255)
    declare @vchServerName    varchar(255)
    declare @vchDatabaseName  varchar(255)
    exec dbo.dt_getpropertiesbyid_vcs @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSProject',       @vchProjectName   OUT
    exec dbo.dt_getpropertiesbyid_vcs @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSSourceSafeINI', @vchSourceSafeINI OUT
    exec dbo.dt_getpropertiesbyid_vcs @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSSQLServer',     @vchServerName    OUT
    exec dbo.dt_getpropertiesbyid_vcs @iPropertyObjectId, 'VCSSQLDatabase',   @vchDatabaseName  OUT

    if @chObjectType = 'PROC'
    begin
        exec @iReturn = master.dbo.sp_OACreate @VSSGUID, @iObjectId OUT

        if @iReturn <> 0 GOTO E_OAError

        declare @vchReturnValue varchar(255)
        select @vchReturnValue = ''

        exec @iReturn = master.dbo.sp_OAMethod @iObjectId,
												'WhoCheckedOut',
												@vchReturnValue OUT,
												@sProjectName = @vchProjectName,
												@sSourceSafeINI = @vchSourceSafeINI,
												@sObjectName = @vchObjectName,
												@sServerName = @vchServerName,
												@sDatabaseName = @vchDatabaseName,
												@sLoginName = @vchLoginName,
												@sPassword = @vchPassword

        if @iReturn <> 0 GOTO E_OAError

        select @vchReturnValue

    end

CleanUp:
    return

E_OAError:
    exec dbo.dt_displayoaerror @iObjectId, @iReturn
    GOTO CleanUp




GO

File: Genius_Database_DNA/2_Database_Code/All_Views.sql
---
-- ==========================================
-- VIEW: [dbo].[v_sales_trans_d]
-- ==========================================
CREATE view [dbo].[v_sales_trans_d] as select sto_id ,itm_id ,sd.qnty ,sd.itm_back ,itm_unit ,sd.sec_insert_date FROM sales_trans_d sd join sales_trans_h sh on sh.sth_id=sd.sth_id union all select std_stock_id sto_id ,itm_id ,sd.qnty ,sd.itm_back ,itm_unit ,sd.sec_insert_date FROM r_sales_trans_d sd

GO

-- ==========================================
-- VIEW: [dbo].[Customer_EInvoice]
-- ==========================================
create view [dbo].[Customer_EInvoice] as
select c.cust_parent as 'CustId',cp.cust_name_ar  ,h.sec_insert_date AS 'TransDate' 
, cast(  sum(case when isnull(fh_contract_id,0)=0 then 	 (( (qnty*itm_sell) * ( (100-itm_dis_per)/100))-itm_dis_mon) * ((100-total_dis_per)/100) 	else  	   	 		case when (qnty*itm_sell)>cc_max_bill_value  			then fh_company_part/no_of_items 		else (qnty*itm_sell)*( (100-itm_dis_per)/100)  			- (  (qnty*itm_sell)*(cc_pay_perc/100) ) 			end 	end 	) as decimal(18,3)) [TransValue] 
FROM sales_trans_d d  	
JOIN sales_trans_h h ON h.sth_id=d.sth_id  
	JOIN Customer c on c.cust_id=h.cust_id 
	Join Customer cp on c.cust_parent=cp.cust_id
		Left JOIN Customer_Contracts cc ON cc.cc_id=h.fh_contract_id 
		 Where sth_flag='C' AND c.cust_parent>0
		 
		 
		  group by cp.cust_name_ar,c.cust_parent,h.sec_insert_date

GO

-- ==========================================
-- VIEW: [dbo].[v_CustomerOpen]
-- ==========================================

   create view v_CustomerOpen as
select cust_id from sales_trans_h 
union all select cust_id  from sales_trans_h where cust_id in (select cust_id from customer where cust_parent=cust_id) 
union all select cust_id from pur_trans_h 
Union all select fh_from_id as cust_id from F_Transaction_Header where fh_from_type=1 
Union all select fh_to_id as cust_id from F_Transaction_Header where fh_to_type=1 
union all select fn_who_id as cust_id from F_Notices where fn_who_type=2

GO

-- ==========================================
-- VIEW: [dbo].[v_vendorOpen]
-- ==========================================

   create view v_vendorOpen as
Select ven_id from pur_trans_h
union all select ibh_ven_id as ven_id from item_back_h
union all select fh_from_id as ven_id from F_Transaction_Header where fh_from_type=2
union all select fh_to_id as ven_id from F_Transaction_Header where fh_to_type=2
union all select fn_who_id as ven_id from F_Notices where fn_who_type=1

GO

-- ==========================================
-- VIEW: [dbo].[v_VenTrans_BillDate]
-- ==========================================
create view [dbo].[v_VenTrans_BillDate] as SELECT 0 Tr,ven_id as VenID ,0 as TransId,sec_insert_date AS TransDate,cast(isnull(ven_start_credit,0) - isnull(ven_start_debit,0) as decimal(18,3)) [TransValue], 'رصيد إفتتاحى' TransType , '' TransNotes , null UserId FROM vendor union all  select 1 Tr,pth2.ven_id as 'VenID' ,pth2.pth_id as 'BillID' ,pth2.ven_bill_date AS 'TransDate', cast( sum (( qnty*((((itm_pur_price*((100-isnull(itm_extra_dis,0))/100))  *((100-isnull(pth2.total_dis_per,0))/100)))+itm_tax_price)) +(itm_tax_price*bonus) - (isnull(pth2.total_des_mon,0)/no_of_items) + (case isnull(p_other_expenses_cost,0) when 1 then (isnull(p_other_expenses,0)/no_of_items) else 0 end))+isnull(sum(qnty*itm_pur_price*(isnull(pth_source_tax,0)/100)),0) as decimal(18,3)) [TransValue], 'فاتوره شراء' TransType  , isnull(ven_bill_no +';','') + pth_notice TransNotes , pth2.sec_insert_uid UserId FROM pur_trans_d ptd join pur_trans_h pth2 on pth2.pth_id=ptd.pth_id  Where pth2.bill_status<>1    group by pth2.ven_id ,pth2.pth_id ,pth2.ven_bill_date ,pth_notice ,pth2.sec_insert_uid,ven_bill_no UNION ALL SELECT 2 Tr,h.ven_id, h.pth_id ,CAST(CONVERT(CHAR(17), d.sec_update_date,113) AS datetime) ,  cast( -1* (isnull(sum(case when (itm_back_qty-itm_back_bonus)<qnty OR bonus=0 then ( (itm_back_qty-itm_back_bonus) * (itm_back_pharm+itm_back_tax)) + (itm_back_bonus*itm_back_tax) else ((qnty * (itm_back_pharm+itm_back_tax)) + ((itm_back_qty-qnty)*itm_back_tax))end ),0)  +isnull(sum((case when itm_back_qty<qnty then itm_back_qty else qnty end)*itm_pur_price*(isnull(pth_source_tax_return,0)/100)),0) ) as decimal(18,3)) [TransValue] , 'مرتجع شراء فاتورة' as BillRet  , pth_notice TransNotes , d.sec_update_uid UserId FROM pur_trans_d d join pur_trans_h h on d.pth_id=h.pth_id  Where itm_back>0 AND bill_status<>1   group by h.pth_id ,h.ven_id ,h.bill_status ,CAST(CONVERT(CHAR(17), d.sec_update_date,113) AS datetime), pth_notice , d.sec_update_uid union all  SELECT 3 Tr,ibh_ven_id as venid ,ibh_id ,ph.sec_insert_date as transdate ,cast( -1* isnull(sum(ibd_qty * (ibd_pharm_price+ibd_tax_price)),0)  as decimal(18,3)) [TransValue] ,'مرتجع شراء عام'  , ibh_notes TransNotes , ph.sec_insert_uid UserId FROM item_back_h ph  join item_back_d pd on ibh_id =ibd_ibh_id  join item_catalog ic on ic.itm_id=ibd_itm_id  group by ibh_ven_id ,ph.sec_insert_date ,ibd_ibh_id ,ibh_id, ibh_notes , ph.sec_insert_uid union all  select 4 Tr,fh_from_id as VenId ,fh_id BillID ,sec_insert_date as TransDate, isnull(sum(fh_value),0) TransValue , 'توريد نقديه' TransType  , fh_notes TransNotes , null UserId from f_transaction_header   where fh_from_type=2 and fh_trans_type=1   group by fh_from_id, fh_id,fh_trans_type, sec_insert_date ,fh_trans_type ,fh_notes union all  select 5 Tr,fh_to_id as VenId ,fh_id as BillID , sec_insert_date as billdate,-1*fh_value TransValue ,'صرف نقديه' TransType  ,fh_notes TransNotes ,h.sec_insert_uid UserId FROM f_transaction_header h   where fh_to_type=2 and fh_trans_type=1 union all  select 6 Tr,fh_from_id VenId,h.fh_id BillID,h.sec_insert_date transdate  ,fh_value TransValue, 'استلام ورقه مالية' TransType  ,fh_notes TransNotes ,h.sec_insert_uid UserId FROM f_transaction_header h join f_transaction_header_ext on f_transaction_header_ext.fh_id=h.fh_id  union all  select 7 Tr,fh_from_id as VenId ,h.fh_id AS BillID, f_transaction_header_ext.sec_update_date as transdate  ,-1* fh_value TransValue , 'الغاء ورقه ماليه مستلمه' TransType  ,fh_notes TransNotes ,h.sec_insert_uid UserId from f_transaction_header h join f_transaction_header_ext on f_transaction_header_ext.fh_id=h.fh_id  union all  select 8 Tr,fh_to_id VenId ,h.fh_id AS BillID,h.sec_insert_date transdate , -1*fh_value TransValue  , 'صرف ورقة مالية' TransType  ,fh_notes TransNotes ,h.sec_insert_uid UserId from f_transaction_header h join f_transaction_header_ext on f_transaction_header_ext.fh_id=h.fh_id   WHERE fh_to_type=2 and fh_trans_type=2 and fhe_start=0    union all  select 9 Tr,fh_to_id VenId,h.fh_id BillID ,f_transaction_header_ext.sec_update_date transdate  ,fh_value TransValue ,'الغاء ورقه ماليه صادره' TransType  ,fh_notes TransNotes ,h.sec_insert_uid UserId from f_transaction_header h  join f_transaction_header_ext on f_transaction_header_ext.fh_id=h.fh_id  WHERE fh_to_type=2 and fh_trans_type=2 and fhe_start=0 and fhe_paper_status ='2'   union all  SELECT 10 Tr, fn_who_id as VenId ,fn_id as BillId ,sec_insert_date Transdate ,fn_value TransVal ,'إشعار إضافة' as TransType  ,fn_notes TransNotes , sec_insert_uid UserId FROM F_Notices  where fn_type=1 and fn_who_type=1 union all  SELECT 11 Tr, fn_who_id as VenId , fn_id as BillId , sec_insert_date as datebill  ,-1*fn_value Debit,'إشعار خصم' TransType  ,fn_notes TransNotes , sec_insert_uid UserId FROM F_Notices where fn_type=2 and fn_who_type=1 and fn_notes<>'تعديل خصومات فواتير شراء'

GO

-- ==========================================
-- VIEW: [dbo].[v_CustTransClass]
-- ==========================================
create view v_CustTransClass as select  t_cust_id,sum(transvalue) t_current_credit from( SELECT tr ,v.custId t_cust_id,transvalue, Transtype trname,TransId,Transdate dt ,case when transvalue<0 then -1*transvalue else 0 end Credit ,case when transvalue>0 then transvalue else 0 end Debit ,0.00 CurrentCredit , 0.00 ItmQty , 0.00 ItmPrice  ,e_Name,Transnotes notes From v_CustTrans v left Join employee e on e.e_id=v.userId  UNION All SELECT 14 tr,a.cust_id,-1*total_cach transvalue,'مكافأة' trname, cou_Id tid, a.sec_insert_date dt ,total_cach Credit, 0.00 Debit,0.000 CurrentCredit , 0.000 ItmQty , 0.000 ItmPrice ,isnull(e_Name,'.') e_Name ,'.' notes FROM Coupons a  LEFT JOIN Employee e on e.e_id= a.sec_insert_uid Where a.bill_id=0  UNION All SELECT 15 tr,a.cust_id, total_cach transvalue,'اصدار كوبون' trname, cou_Id tid, a.sec_insert_date dt ,0.00 Credit, total_cach Debit,0.000 CurrentCredit , 0.000 ItmQty , 0.000 ItmPrice ,isnull(e_Name,'.') e_Name ,'.' notes FROM Coupons a  LEFT JOIN Employee e on e.e_id= a.sec_insert_uid  union ALL  select 16 tr,s.cust_id,-1*sc_used_value transvalue,'سداد كوبون' trname, a.sth_id tid,  DATEADD([SECOND], - 2, a.sec_insert_date) dt ,sc_used_value Credit,0.00  Debit,0.000 CurrentCredit , 0.000 ItmQty , 0.000 ItmPrice ,isnull(e_Name,'.') e_Name ,'.' notes FROM sales_cobons a  LEFT JOIN Employee e on e.e_id= a.sec_insert_uid  Inner Join sales_trans_h s on s.sth_id=a.sth_id  union ALL select  17 tr,a.cust_id,-1*total_cach transvalue,'رد كوبون' trname, cou_Id tid,  DATEADD([SECOND], - 1, a.sec_insert_date) dt ,total_cach Credit, 0.00 Debit,0.000 CurrentCredit , 0.000 ItmQty , 0.000 ItmPrice ,isnull(e_Name,'.') e_Name ,'.' notes  FROM Coupons a  LEFT JOIN Employee e on e.e_id= a.sec_insert_uid  Where a.total_cach<>a.total_bill_cash and a.bill_id<>0  UNION All SELECT 18 tr,a.cust_id,total_cach transvalue,'الغاء مكافأة' trname, cou_Id tid, a.sec_update_date dt ,0.00 Credit, total_cach Debit,0.000 CurrentCredit , 0.000 ItmQty , 0.000 ItmPrice ,isnull(e_Name,'.') e_Name ,'.' notes FROM Coupons a  LEFT JOIN Employee e on e.e_id= a.sec_insert_uid Where a.status='R'  UNION All SELECT 19 tr,a.cust_id,-1*total_cach transvalue,'الغاء كوبون' trname, cou_Id tid, a.sec_update_date dt , total_cach Credit,0.00 Debit,0.000 CurrentCredit , 0.000 ItmQty , 0.000 ItmPrice ,isnull(e_Name,'.') e_Name ,'.' notes FROM Coupons a  LEFT JOIN Employee e on e.e_id= a.sec_insert_uid Where a.status <>'N' union ALL  SELECT 20 tr,a.cust_id,-1*visa_value transvalue,'تحصيل بطاقة الأئتمان' trname, sth_id tid, a.sec_update_date dt ,visa_value Credit, 0.00 Debit,0.000 CurrentCredit , 0.000 ItmQty , 0.000 ItmPrice ,isnull(e_Name,'.') e_Name ,'.' notes FROM sales_visa_info a LEFT JOIN Employee e on e.e_id= a.sec_insert_uid Where fh_id<>0  union ALL  SELECT 21 tr,a.cust_id,visa_return_value transvalue,'مرتجع بطاقة الأئتمان' trname, sth_id tid, a.sec_update_date dt ,0.00 Credit, visa_return_value Debit,0.000 CurrentCredit , 0.000 ItmQty , 0.000 ItmPrice ,isnull(e_Name,'.') e_Name ,'.' notes FROM sales_visa_info a LEFT JOIN Employee e on e.e_id= a.sec_update_uid Where visa_return_value<>0  ) customertrans where t_cust_id<>0 group by t_cust_id

GO

-- ==========================================
-- VIEW: [dbo].[v_CustTrans]
-- ==========================================
CREATE view [dbo].[v_CustTrans]   AS  
SELECT 0 Tr,cust_id as CustId ,0 as TransId,sec_insert_date AS TransDate,    cast(isnull(cust_start_debit,0) - isnull(cust_start_credit,0) as decimal(18,3)) [TransValue]   , 'رصيد إفتتاحى' TransType , '' TransNotes , null UserId , 0 TransSubType FROM Customer
union all   select 1 Tr,h.cust_id as 'CustId' ,h.sth_id as 'BillID' ,h.sec_insert_date AS 'TransDate' , cast(  sum(case when isnull(fh_contract_id,0)=0 then 	 (( (qnty*itm_sell) * ( (100-itm_dis_per)/100))-itm_dis_mon) * ((100-total_dis_per)/100) 	else  		case when (qnty*itm_sell)>cc_max_bill_value  			then (cc_max_bill_value*(cc_pay_perc/100))+((qnty*itm_sell)-cc_max_bill_value)  		else (qnty*itm_sell)*(cc_pay_perc/100)  			end 	end 	) +isnull((select isnull(sth_extra_expenses,0)-isnull(total_des_mon,0) From sales_trans_h h2 Where h2.sth_id=h.sth_id and h2.bill_typ<>5),0) as decimal(18,3)) [TransValue] , 'بيع' TransType  ,sth_notice TransNotes , h.sec_insert_uid UserId ,h.bill_typ TransSubType FROM sales_trans_d d  	JOIN sales_trans_h h ON h.sth_id=d.sth_id  	JOIN Customer c on c.cust_id=h.cust_id 	Left JOIN Customer_Contracts cc ON cc.cc_id=h.fh_contract_id WHERE sth_flag='C'  group by h.cust_id, h.sth_Id,h.sec_insert_date, sth_notice, h.sec_insert_uid,bill_typ 
UNION ALL  select 2 Tr,h.cust_id as 'CustId' ,h.sth_id as 'BillID' ,d.sec_update_date AS 'TransDate' ,-1* cast(  sum(case when isnull(fh_contract_id,0)=0 then 	 (( (itm_back * itm_back_price) * ( (100-itm_dis_per)/100))-itm_dis_mon) * ((100-total_dis_per)/100) 	else  	     (itm_back * itm_back_price)*(cc_pay_perc/100)  	end 	) as decimal(18,3)) [TransValue] , 'مرتجع بيع' TransType  , sth_notice TransNotes , d.sec_update_uid UserId ,h.bill_typ TransSubType FROM sales_trans_d d  	JOIN sales_trans_h h ON h.sth_id=d.sth_id  	JOIN Customer c on c.cust_id=h.cust_id 	Left JOIN Customer_Contracts cc ON cc.cc_id=h.fh_contract_id  Where sth_flag='C' group by h.cust_id, h.sth_Id, d.sec_update_uid , sth_notice, d.sec_update_date,bill_typ having sum(itm_back * itm_back_price)>0 
union all    SELECT 3 Tr,cust_id as CustId ,h.pth_id  ,h.sec_insert_date as transdate ,cast(-1* total_bill as decimal(18,3)) [TransValue]  ,'مرتجع بيع عام' TransType , cast(pth_notice as varchar(300)) TransNotes , h.sec_insert_uid UserId ,0 TransSubType  FROM pur_trans_h h    WHERE pth_sell_back=1  AND isnull(h.cust_id,0)>0 
UNION ALL  select 1 Tr,c.cust_parent as 'CustId' ,h.sth_id as 'BillID' ,h.sec_insert_date AS 'TransDate' , cast(  sum(case when isnull(fh_contract_id,0)=0 then 	 (( (qnty*itm_sell) * ( (100-itm_dis_per)/100))-itm_dis_mon) * ((100-total_dis_per)/100) 	else  	   	 		case when (qnty*itm_sell)>cc_max_bill_value  			then fh_company_part/no_of_items 		else (qnty*itm_sell)*( (100-itm_dis_per)/100)  			- (  (qnty*itm_sell)*(cc_pay_perc/100) ) 			end 	end 	) as decimal(18,3)) [TransValue] , 'بيع'  , sth_notice TransNotes , h.sec_insert_uid UserId ,bill_typ TransSubType FROM sales_trans_d d  	JOIN sales_trans_h h ON h.sth_id=d.sth_id  	JOIN Customer c on c.cust_id=h.cust_id 	Left JOIN Customer_Contracts cc ON cc.cc_id=h.fh_contract_id  Where sth_flag='C' AND cust_parent>0 group by c.cust_parent, h.sth_Id,h.sec_insert_date, sth_notice, h.sec_insert_uid,bill_typ 
UNION ALL  select 2 Tr,c.cust_parent as 'CustId' ,h.sth_id as 'BillID' ,d.sec_update_date AS 'TransDate' ,-1* cast(  sum(case when isnull(fh_contract_id,0)=0 then 	 (( (itm_back * itm_back_price) * ( (100-itm_dis_per)/100))-itm_dis_mon) * ((100-total_dis_per)/100) 	else  	   	 (itm_back * itm_back_price)*( (100-itm_dis_per)/100)  			- (  (itm_back * itm_back_price)*(cc_pay_perc/100) ) 	end 	) as decimal(18,3)) [TransValue] , 'مرتجع بيع'  , sth_notice TransNotes , h.sec_insert_uid UserId ,bill_typ TransSubType FROM sales_trans_d d  	JOIN sales_trans_h h ON h.sth_id=d.sth_id  	JOIN Customer c on c.cust_id=h.cust_id 	Left JOIN Customer_Contracts cc ON cc.cc_id=h.fh_contract_id  Where sth_flag='C' AND cust_parent>0 group by c.cust_parent, h.sth_Id,d.sec_update_date, sth_notice, h.sec_insert_uid,bill_typ having sum (itm_back * itm_back_price)>0 
union all   select 4 Tr,fh_from_id as CustId  ,fh_id BillID ,sec_insert_date as TransDate, -1*fh_value TransValue , 'توريد نقديه' TransType   , fh_notes TransNotes , h.sec_insert_uid UserId ,0 TransSubType from f_transaction_header h  where fh_from_type=1 and fh_trans_type=1  
union all   select 5 Tr,fh_to_id as CustId ,fh_id as BillID , sec_insert_date as billdate,fh_value TransValue ,'صرف نقديه' TransType   ,fh_notes TransNotes ,h.sec_insert_uid UserId ,0 TransSubType FROM f_transaction_header h where fh_to_type=1 and fh_trans_type=1   
union all   select 6 Tr,fh_from_id CustId,h.fh_id BillID,h.sec_insert_date transdate   ,-1*fh_value TransValue, 'استلام ورقه مالية' TransType   ,fh_notes TransNotes ,h.sec_insert_uid UserId ,0 TransSubType FROM f_transaction_header h  join f_transaction_header_ext d on d.fh_id=h.fh_id where fh_from_type=1 and fh_trans_type=2 and fhe_start=0 
union all   select 7 Tr,fh_from_id as CustId ,h.fh_id AS BillID, d.sec_update_date as transdate    ,fh_value TransValue , 'الغاء ورقه ماليه مستلمه' TransType   ,fh_notes TransNotes ,h.sec_insert_uid UserId ,0 TransSubType  from f_transaction_header h join f_transaction_header_ext d on d.fh_id=h.fh_id    where fh_from_type=1 and fh_trans_type=2 and fhe_paper_status='2' and fhe_start=0 
union all   select 8 Tr,fh_to_id CustId ,h.fh_id AS BillID,h.sec_insert_date transdate , fh_value TransValue    , 'صرف ورقة مالية' TransType   ,fh_notes TransNotes ,h.sec_insert_uid UserId ,0 TransSubType from f_transaction_header h join f_transaction_header_ext d on d.fh_id=h.fh_id      WHERE fh_to_type=1 and fh_trans_type=2 and fhe_start=0 
union all   select 9 Tr,fh_to_id CustId,h.fh_id BillID ,d.sec_update_date transdate   ,-1*fh_value TransValue ,'الغاء ورقه ماليه صادره' TransType   ,fh_notes TransNotes ,h.sec_insert_uid UserId ,0 TransSubType from f_transaction_header h     join f_transaction_header_ext d on d.fh_id=h.fh_id    WHERE fh_to_type=1 and fh_trans_type=2 AND fhe_paper_status in ('2','3') and fhe_start=0 
union all   SELECT 10 Tr, fn_who_id as CustId ,fn_id as BillId ,sec_insert_date Transdate  ,fn_value  TransVal  ,'إشعار إضافة' as TransType   ,fn_notes TransNotes , sec_insert_uid UserId ,0 TransSubType  FROM F_Notices    where fn_type=1 and fn_who_type=2  
union all    SELECT 11 Tr, fn_who_id as CustId , fn_id as BillId , sec_insert_date as datebill     ,-1*fn_value Debit,'إشعار خصم' TransType     ,fn_notes TransNotes , sec_insert_uid UserId ,0 TransSubType  FROM F_Notices  where fn_type=2 and fn_who_type=2
UNION ALL  select 12 Tr,h.cust_id as 'CustId' ,h.sth_id as 'BillID' ,d.sec_insert_date AS 'TransDate' ,-1* d.sth_extra_expenses_back  [TransValue] , 'مرتجع مصاريف إضافية' TransType  , sth_notice TransNotes , d.sec_insert_uid UserId ,h.bill_typ TransSubType FROM sales_return d  JOIN sales_trans_h h ON h.sth_id=d.sth_id JOIN Customer c on c.cust_id=h.cust_id Left JOIN Customer_Contracts cc ON cc.cc_id=h.fh_contract_id  Where sth_flag='C' group by h.cust_id, h.sth_Id, d.sec_insert_uid , sth_notice, d.sec_insert_date,bill_typ,d.sth_extra_expenses_back having d.sth_extra_expenses_back>0

GO

-- ==========================================
-- VIEW: [dbo].[v_ItemsTrans]
-- ==========================================
Create View [dbo].[v_ItemsTrans] as   	select 1 t_id,'Pur' t_text,s.sto_id t_sto_Id,ic.itm_id t_itm_id	,cast(isnull(sum((ptd.qnty+ptd.bonus-itm_back_qty) * (case itm_purchase_unit when 3 then 1  when 2 then (itm_unit1_unit3/itm_unit1_unit2) when 1 then itm_unit1_unit3 end)) ,0) as decimal(18,2)) t_qty  	FROM item_catalog ic join pur_trans_d ptd on ptd.itm_id=ic.itm_id  		JOIN  pur_trans_h pth on pth.pth_id= ptd.pth_id and bill_status<>1  		Join Store s on s.sto_id=pth.sto_id and activated=1 	Group by s.sto_id,ic.itm_id union all 	select 2 t_id,'PurRetGen' t_text,ibd_s_id,ic.itm_id ,cast(-1*isnull(sum(ptd.ibd_qty * (case itm_purchase_unit when 3 then 1 when 2 then (itm_unit1_unit3/itm_unit1_unit2) when 1 then itm_unit1_unit3 end)),0) as decimal(18,2)) GENRetPurchasedQTY 	from item_catalog ic join Item_back_d ptd on ptd.ibd_itm_id=ic.itm_id  		Join Store s on s.sto_id=ibd_s_id and activated=1 	Where ptd.ibd_qty>0 group by ibd_s_id,ic.itm_id union all 	select 4 t_id,'Sales' t_text,s.sto_id, ic.itm_id,cast(-1*isnull(sum((std.qnty-itm_back) * case itm_unit when 3 then 1 when 2 then (itm_unit1_unit3/itm_unit1_unit2) else itm_unit1_unit3 end),0) as decimal(18,2)) SOLDQTY 	From item_catalog ic join sales_trans_d std on std.itm_id=ic.itm_id JOIN sales_trans_h sth on sth.sth_id=std.sth_id  	Join Store s on s.sto_id=sth.sto_id and activated=1 	Where itm_nexist<>1  and isnull(itm_srvc,0)=0 	Group by s.sto_id, ic.itm_id union all 	select 6 t_id,'TransFrom' t_text, st_from_store, ic.itm_id,-1* isnull(sum(st_itm_quantity),0) TransFrom  	from item_catalog ic join store_trans st on st.st_itm_id=ic.itm_id 	Join Store s on s.sto_id=st_from_store and activated=1 	Where isnull(stnh_id,0)=0  	or exists (select * from Store_Trans_h h WHERE h.stnh_id=st.stnh_id and stnh_f_Sto_id=st_from_store  	AND (stnh_flag in ('R','S','A') or (stnh_flag ='P' and isnull(stnh_pend_affect_stk,0)=1))) And isnull(st_c_id,0)>0 	group by st_from_store, ic.itm_id union all 	select 3 t_id,'TransTo' t_text, st_to_store, ic.itm_id, isnull(sum(st_itm_quantity),0) TransTo 	 	from item_catalog ic join store_trans st on st.st_itm_id=ic.itm_id  		Join Store s on s.sto_id=st_to_store and activated=1 	Where isnull(stnh_id,0)=0 or exists (select * from Store_Trans_h h WHERE stnh_flag in ('R','A') and h.stnh_id=st.stnh_id and stnh_f_sto_id=st_from_store) And isnull(st_c_id,0)>0 	group by st_to_store, ic.itm_id union all 	select 5 t_id,'BeginSock' t_text, s.sto_id, itm_id, isnull(sum(itm_qty),0) BeginSock  	From item_class k Join Store s on s.sto_id=k.stock_id and activated=1 	Where active_flg='B' 	Group by s.sto_id, itm_id union all 	select 7 t_id,'Taswyya' t_text, iqc_store_id,iqc_itm_id,isnull(sum(iqc_new_quantity-iqc_old_quantity),0) Taswyya 	from Item_Quantity_Changes st 	Join Store s on s.sto_id=iqc_store_id and activated=1 	Group by iqc_store_id,iqc_itm_id union all 	select 9 t_id,'Inventory' t_text,inv_sto_id, std.invd_itm_id,cast(isnull(sum((invd_new_qty-invd_old_qty) *(case invd_itm_unit when 3 then 1 when 2 then  (itm_unit1_unit3/itm_unit1_unit2) else itm_unit1_unit3 end)),0) as decimal(18,2)) [qnty]  	FROM item_catalog ic join Inventory_d std on std.invd_itm_id=ic.itm_id JOIN Inventory_h sth on sth.inv_id=std.invd_inv_id  	Join Store s on s.sto_id=inv_sto_id and activated=1 	Where sth.inv_flag='C' and invd_new_qty-invd_old_qty<>0  	Group by  inv_sto_id, std.invd_itm_id

GO

-- ==========================================
-- VIEW: [dbo].[v_ItemsTransClass]
-- ==========================================
CREATE View [dbo].[v_ItemsTransClass] As select 1 t_id,'Pur' t_text,s.sto_id t_sto_Id,ic.itm_id t_itm_id,ptd.c_id t_c_id,cast( isnull(sum((ptd.qnty+ptd.bonus-itm_back_qty) * (case itm_purchase_unit when 3 then 1 when 2 then (itm_unit1_unit3/itm_unit1_unit2) when 1 then itm_unit1_unit3 end)) ,0) as decimal(18,2)) t_Qty    ,exp_date t_exp_date  FROM item_catalog ic join pur_trans_d ptd on ptd.itm_id=ic.itm_id   JOIN pur_trans_h pth on pth.pth_id= ptd.pth_id and bill_status<>1   Join Store s on s.sto_id=pth.sto_id and activated=1   Where isnull(c_id,0)>0   Group by s.sto_id,ic.itm_id,ptd.c_id,exp_date union all   select 2 t_id,'PurRetGen' t_text,ibd_s_id,ic.itm_id,ptd.ibd_c_id,cast( -1*isnull(sum(ptd.ibd_qty * (case itm_purchase_unit when 3 then 1 when 2 then (itm_unit1_unit3/itm_unit1_unit2) when 1 then itm_unit1_unit3 end)),0) as decimal(18,2)) GENRetPurchasedQTY,ibd_exp_date t_exp_date  FROM item_catalog ic    join Item_back_d ptd on ptd.ibd_itm_id=ic.itm_id    Join Store s on s.sto_id=ibd_s_id and activated=1    Where ptd.ibd_qty>0    group by ibd_s_id,ic.itm_id,ptd.ibd_c_id,ibd_exp_date UNION ALL  select 4 t_id,'Sales' t_text,s.sto_id, ic.itm_id,case when c_id<>0 then c_id else (select Max(c_id) from item_class_store where ic.itm_id=item_class_store.itm_id and item_class_store.sto_id=s.sto_id )end c_id,cast(-1*isnull(sum((std.qnty-itm_back) * case itm_unit when 3 then 1   when 2 then (itm_unit1_unit3/itm_unit1_unit2)   else itm_unit1_unit3 end),0) as decimal(18,2)) SOLDQTY ,cast(exp_date as datetime) t_exp_date   From item_catalog ic join sales_trans_d std on std.itm_id=ic.itm_id join sales_trans_h sth on sth.sth_id=std.sth_id    Join Store s on s.sto_id=sth.sto_id and activated=1   Where itm_nexist<>1    and isnull(itm_srvc,0)=0   Group by s.sto_id, ic.itm_id,c_id,cast(exp_date as datetime) UNION ALL  select 6 t_id,'TransFrom' t_text, st_from_store, ic.itm_id, st_c_id,-1*isnull(sum(st_itm_quantity),0) TransFrom,st_itm_expiry t_exp_date   FROM item_catalog ic join store_trans st on st.st_itm_id=ic.itm_id    Join Store s on s.sto_id=st_from_store and activated=1   Where isnull(stnh_id,0)=0     or exists (select * from Store_Trans_h h WHERE h.stnh_id=st.stnh_id and stnh_f_Sto_id=st_from_store     AND (stnh_flag in ('R','S','A') or (stnh_flag ='P' and isnull(stnh_pend_affect_stk,0)=1)))   group by st_from_store, ic.itm_id, st_c_id,st_itm_expiry UNION ALL  select 3 t_id,'TransTo' t_text, st_to_store, ic.itm_id, st_c_id, isnull(sum(st_itm_quantity),0) TransTo ,st_itm_expiry t_exp_date   FROM item_catalog ic join store_trans st on st.st_itm_id=ic.itm_id    Join Store s on s.sto_id=st_to_store and activated=1     Where ( isnull(stnh_id,0)=0  or stnh_id in (select stnh_id from Store_Trans_h WHERE stnh_flag in ('R','A') and stnh_f_Sto_id=st_from_store and stnh_t_Sto_id=st_to_store))   group by st_to_store, ic.itm_id, st_c_id,st_itm_expiry UNION ALL  select 5 t_id,'BeginSock' t_text, s.sto_id, itm_id, c_id, isnull(sum(itm_qty),0) BeginSock , null t_exp_date  From item_class k Join Store s on s.sto_id=k.stock_id and activated=1  Where active_flg='B'  Group by s.sto_id, itm_id, c_id UNION ALL  select 7 t_id,'Taswyya' t_text, iqc_store_id,iqc_itm_id,iqc_c_id, sum(isnull(iqc_new_quantity,0) - isnull(iqc_old_quantity,0)) Taswyya ,iqc_expiry  FROM Item_Quantity_Changes st    Join Store s on s.sto_id=iqc_store_id and activated=1  Group by iqc_store_id,iqc_itm_id,iqc_c_id,iqc_expiry UNION ALL  select 9 t_id,'Inventory' t_text,inv_sto_id, std.invd_itm_id,case when invd_c_id<>0 then invd_c_id else (select Max(c_id) from item_class_store where std.invd_itm_id=item_class_store.itm_id and item_class_store.sto_id=inv_sto_id )end invd_c_id, isnull(sum((invd_new_qty-invd_old_qty) *(case invd_itm_unit when 3 then 1 when 2 then (itm_unit1_unit3/itm_unit1_unit2) else itm_unit1_unit3 end)),0) [qnty] ,invd_new_exp_date  FROM item_catalog ic JOIN Inventory_d std on std.invd_itm_id=ic.itm_id   JOIN Inventory_h sth on sth.inv_id=std.invd_inv_id   Join Store s on s.sto_id=inv_sto_id and activated=1  Where sth.inv_flag='C' and invd_new_qty-invd_old_qty<>0 Group by inv_sto_id, std.invd_itm_id,invd_c_id,invd_new_exp_date

GO

-- ==========================================
-- VIEW: [dbo].[v_VenTrans]
-- ==========================================
CREATE view [dbo].[v_VenTrans] as SELECT 0 Tr,ven_id as VenID ,0 as TransId,sec_insert_date AS TransDate,cast(isnull(ven_start_credit,0) - isnull(ven_start_debit,0) as decimal(18,3)) [TransValue], 'رصيد إفتتاحى' TransType , '' TransNotes , null UserId FROM vendor union all  select 1 Tr,pth2.ven_id as 'VenID' ,pth2.pth_id as 'BillID' ,pth2.sec_insert_date AS 'TransDate', cast( sum (( qnty*((((itm_pur_price*((100-isnull(itm_extra_dis,0))/100))  *((100-isnull(pth2.total_dis_per,0))/100)))+itm_tax_price)) +(itm_tax_price*bonus) - (isnull(pth2.total_des_mon,0)/no_of_items) + (case isnull(p_other_expenses_cost,0) when 1 then (isnull(p_other_expenses,0)/no_of_items) else 0 end))+isnull(sum(qnty*itm_pur_price*(isnull(pth_source_tax,0)/100)),0) as decimal(18,3)) [TransValue], 'فاتوره شراء' TransType  , isnull(ven_bill_no +';','') + pth_notice TransNotes , pth2.sec_insert_uid UserId FROM pur_trans_d ptd join pur_trans_h pth2 on pth2.pth_id=ptd.pth_id  Where pth2.bill_status<>1    group by pth2.ven_id ,pth2.pth_id ,pth2.sec_insert_date ,pth_notice ,pth2.sec_insert_uid,ven_bill_no UNION ALL SELECT 2 Tr,h.ven_id, h.pth_id ,CAST(CONVERT(CHAR(17), d.sec_update_date,113) AS datetime) ,  cast( -1* (isnull(sum(case when (itm_back_qty-itm_back_bonus)<qnty OR bonus=0 then ( (itm_back_qty-itm_back_bonus) * (itm_back_pharm+itm_back_tax)) + (itm_back_bonus*itm_back_tax) else ((qnty * (itm_back_pharm+itm_back_tax)) + ((itm_back_qty-qnty)*itm_back_tax))end ),0)  +isnull(sum((case when itm_back_qty<qnty then itm_back_qty else qnty end)*itm_pur_price*(isnull(pth_source_tax_return,0)/100)),0) ) as decimal(18,3)) [TransValue] , 'مرتجع شراء فاتورة' as BillRet  , pth_notice TransNotes , d.sec_update_uid UserId FROM pur_trans_d d join pur_trans_h h on d.pth_id=h.pth_id  Where itm_back>0 AND bill_status<>1   group by h.pth_id ,h.ven_id ,h.bill_status ,CAST(CONVERT(CHAR(17), d.sec_update_date,113) AS datetime), pth_notice , d.sec_update_uid union all  SELECT 3 Tr,ibh_ven_id as venid ,ibh_id ,ph.sec_insert_date as transdate ,cast( -1* isnull(sum(ibd_qty * (ibd_pharm_price+ibd_tax_price)),0)  as decimal(18,3)) [TransValue] ,'مرتجع شراء عام'  , ibh_notes TransNotes , ph.sec_insert_uid UserId FROM item_back_h ph  join item_back_d pd on ibh_id =ibd_ibh_id  join item_catalog ic on ic.itm_id=ibd_itm_id  group by ibh_ven_id ,ph.sec_insert_date ,ibd_ibh_id ,ibh_id, ibh_notes , ph.sec_insert_uid union all  select 4 Tr,fh_from_id as VenId ,fh_id BillID ,sec_insert_date as TransDate, isnull(sum(fh_value),0) TransValue , 'توريد نقديه' TransType  , fh_notes TransNotes , null UserId from f_transaction_header   where fh_from_type=2 and fh_trans_type=1   group by fh_from_id, fh_id,fh_trans_type, sec_insert_date ,fh_trans_type ,fh_notes union all  select 5 Tr,fh_to_id as VenId ,fh_id as BillID , sec_insert_date as billdate,-1*fh_value TransValue ,'صرف نقديه' TransType  ,fh_notes TransNotes ,h.sec_insert_uid UserId FROM f_transaction_header h   where fh_to_type=2 and fh_trans_type=1 union all  select 6 Tr,fh_from_id VenId,h.fh_id BillID,h.sec_insert_date transdate  ,fh_value TransValue, 'استلام ورقه مالية' TransType  ,fh_notes TransNotes ,h.sec_insert_uid UserId FROM f_transaction_header h join f_transaction_header_ext on f_transaction_header_ext.fh_id=h.fh_id  union all  select 7 Tr,fh_from_id as VenId ,h.fh_id AS BillID, f_transaction_header_ext.sec_update_date as transdate  ,-1* fh_value TransValue , 'الغاء ورقه ماليه مستلمه' TransType  ,fh_notes TransNotes ,h.sec_insert_uid UserId from f_transaction_header h join f_transaction_header_ext on f_transaction_header_ext.fh_id=h.fh_id  union all  select 8 Tr,fh_to_id VenId ,h.fh_id AS BillID,h.sec_insert_date transdate , -1*fh_value TransValue  , 'صرف ورقة مالية' TransType  ,fh_notes TransNotes ,h.sec_insert_uid UserId from f_transaction_header h join f_transaction_header_ext on f_transaction_header_ext.fh_id=h.fh_id   WHERE fh_to_type=2 and fh_trans_type=2 and fhe_start=0    union all  select 9 Tr,fh_to_id VenId,h.fh_id BillID ,f_transaction_header_ext.sec_update_date transdate  ,fh_value TransValue ,'الغاء ورقه ماليه صادره' TransType  ,fh_notes TransNotes ,h.sec_insert_uid UserId from f_transaction_header h  join f_transaction_header_ext on f_transaction_header_ext.fh_id=h.fh_id  WHERE fh_to_type=2 and fh_trans_type=2 and fhe_start=0 and fhe_paper_status ='2'   union all  SELECT 10 Tr, fn_who_id as VenId ,fn_id as BillId ,sec_insert_date Transdate ,fn_value TransVal ,'إشعار إضافة' as TransType  ,fn_notes TransNotes , sec_insert_uid UserId FROM F_Notices  where fn_type=1 and fn_who_type=1 union all  SELECT 11 Tr, fn_who_id as VenId , fn_id as BillId , sec_insert_date as datebill  ,-1*fn_value Debit,'إشعار خصم' TransType  ,fn_notes TransNotes , sec_insert_uid UserId FROM F_Notices where fn_type=2 and fn_who_type=1 and fn_notes<>'تعديل خصومات فواتير شراء'

GO




File: Genius_Database_DNA/2_Database_Code/All_Triggers.sql
---
-- ==========================================
-- TRIGGER: [dbo].[UPDATEDATE] ON TABLE [Customer]
-- ==========================================
CREATE TRIGGER [UPDATEDATE] ON [dbo].[Customer] 
FOR UPDATE
AS

IF  UPDATE(cust_current_credit) 
BEGIN
	DECLARE @id decimal(18,0)
	select @id=(SELECT cust_id FROM inserted)
	update customer set sec_update_date=getdate() where cust_id=@id
END 





GO

-- ==========================================
-- TRIGGER: [dbo].[VUPDATEDATE] ON TABLE [Vendor]
-- ==========================================
CREATE TRIGGER [VUPDATEDATE] ON [dbo].[vendor] 
FOR UPDATE
AS
IF  UPDATE(ven_current_credit) 
BEGIN
	DECLARE @id decimal(18,0)
	select @id=(SELECT ven_id FROM inserted)
	update vendor set sec_update_date=getdate() where ven_id=@id
END 







GO

-- ==========================================
-- TRIGGER: [dbo].[delete_duplicate_records] ON TABLE [F_Transaction_Header]
-- ==========================================
create TRIGGER [dbo].[delete_duplicate_records] ON [dbo].[f_transaction_header] 
FOR INSERT, UPDATE 
AS 
DECLARE @fh_notes VARCHAR(200) 
DECLARE @fh_trans_type decimal(18,0) 
DECLARE @fh_trans_type2 decimal(18,0)
DECLARE @fh_form_type decimal(18,0)
DECLARE @fh_id decimal(18,0)

select @fh_notes = (SELECT fh_notes FROM inserted)
select @fh_trans_type = (SELECT fh_trans_type FROM inserted)
select @fh_trans_type2 = (SELECT fh_trans_type2 FROM inserted)
select @fh_form_type = (SELECT fh_form_type FROM inserted)
select @fh_id = (SELECT fh_id FROM inserted)

if( @fh_trans_type=1 and @fh_trans_type2=1 and @fh_form_type=1 and @fh_notes like '%فاتورة بيع  رقم%')
begin
if not exists (select * from sysobjects where name='F_Transaction_Header_SaveDeleteRecords' and xtype='U')
CREATE TABLE [dbo].[F_Transaction_Header_SaveDeleteRecords] (
	[fh_id] [decimal](18, 0)  NULL ,
	[fh_trans_type] [varchar] (2) COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1256_CI_AS NULL ,
	[fh_trans_type2] [varchar] (2) COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1256_CI_AS NULL ,
	[fh_code] [varchar] (10) COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1256_CI_AS NULL ,
	[fh_value] [money] NULL ,
	[fh_from_type] [varchar] (2) COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1256_CI_AS NULL ,
	[fh_from_id] [decimal](18, 0) NULL ,
	[fh_to_type] [varchar] (2) COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1256_CI_AS NULL ,
	[fh_to_id] [decimal](18, 0) NULL ,
	[fh_notes] [varchar] (250) COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1256_CI_AS NULL ,
	[fh_actual_date] [datetime] NULL ,
	[fh_computer] [varchar] (100) COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1256_CI_AS NULL ,
	[fh_actual_cashier_id] [decimal](18, 0) NULL ,
	[fh_form_type] [int] NULL ,
	[sec_insert_uid] [varchar] (50) COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1256_CI_AS NULL ,
	[sec_insert_date] [datetime] NULL ,
	[sec_update_uid] [varchar] (50) COLLATE SQL_Latin1_General_CP1256_CI_AS NULL ,
	[sec_update_date] [datetime] NULL ,
	[fh_sto_id] [decimal](18, 0) NULL ,
	[fh_cost_sto_id] [int] NULL 
)
Insert into F_Transaction_Header_SaveDeleteRecords select * from f_transaction_header where fh_trans_type=1 and fh_trans_type2=1 and fh_form_type=1 and fh_notes=@fh_notes and fh_id<>@fh_id
   delete from f_transaction_header where fh_trans_type=1 and fh_trans_type2=1 and fh_form_type=1 and fh_notes=@fh_notes and fh_id<>@fh_id
end

GO

-- ==========================================
-- TRIGGER: [dbo].[watch_stock] ON TABLE [Item_Class_Store]
-- ==========================================
---//---//---//---//---//---//---//---//---//---//---//---//---//---//---//---//---//---//---//---//---//---//---//
Create TRIGGER watch_stock ON Item_Class_Store 
FOR INSERT, UPDATE 
AS 
DECLARE @AppName VARCHAR(200) 
DECLARE @PCName VARCHAR(200) 
DECLARE @StoreId decimal(18,0) 
DECLARE @ItemId decimal(18,0) 
DECLARE @ClassId decimal(18,0) 
DECLARE @OldQty decimal(18,0) 
DECLARE @NewQty decimal(18,0) 
DECLARE @itm_exp datetime 
select @AppName = (SELECT top 1 program_name from master.dbo.sysprocesses (nolock) where spid=@@spid) 
select @PCName = (SELECT top 1 hostname from master.dbo.sysprocesses (nolock) where spid=@@spid) 
IF EXISTS (select * from deleted) 
BEGIN 
	IF UPDATE(itm_qty) or UPDATE(itm_expiry_date)
	BEGIN 
		select @StoreId = (SELECT sto_id FROM inserted) 		
		if EXISTS (select sto_id from store where sto_id=@StoreId and activated<>2) 
		Begin
			select @ItemId = (SELECT itm_id FROM inserted) 
			select @ClassId = (SELECT c_id FROM inserted) 
			select @OldQty = (SELECT itm_qty FROM deleted) 
			select @NewQty = (SELECT itm_qty FROM inserted) 
			select @itm_exp = (SELECT itm_expiry_date FROM deleted)
			insert into watch_qty_chng (c_id,itm_id,sto_id,old_qty,new_qty,prog_nm,pc_nm,oper,itm_expiry_date) 
				values (@ClassId,@ItemId ,@StoreId,@OldQty,@NewQty,@AppName,@PCName,'UPDATE',@itm_exp) 
		END
	END 
END 
ELSE 
BEGIN 
	IF EXISTS (select * from inserted) 
	BEGIN 
		select @StoreId = (SELECT sto_id FROM inserted) 
		if EXISTS (select sto_id from store where sto_id=@StoreId and activated<>2)
		Begin
			select @ItemId = (SELECT itm_id FROM inserted) 
			select @ClassId = (SELECT c_id FROM inserted) 
			select @OldQty = (SELECT isnull(itm_qty,0) FROM deleted) 
			select @NewQty = (SELECT itm_qty FROM inserted) 
			select @itm_exp = (SELECT itm_expiry_date FROM deleted)
			insert into watch_qty_chng (c_id,itm_id,sto_id,old_qty,new_qty,prog_nm,pc_nm,oper,itm_expiry_date) 
				values (@ClassId,@ItemId ,@StoreId,@OldQty,@NewQty,@AppName,@PCName,'INSERT',@itm_exp) 
		End
	END 
END


GO

-- ==========================================
-- TRIGGER: [dbo].[close_stock] ON TABLE [Item_Class_Store]
-- ==========================================
Create TRIGGER [close_stock] ON [Item_Class_Store] After UPDATE, Insert 
AS 
DECLARE @StoreId decimal(18,0) 
select @StoreId = (SELECT top 1 sto_id FROM inserted) 

if EXISTS (select sto_id from store where sto_id=@StoreId and activated<>2)
Begin
	------ ---------- --------------- SaveMonthClose
	declare @lastdate as datetime
	declare @currdate as datetime
	set @currdate= getdate()
	set @lastdate =(select isnull(last_month_close_dt,'2013/05/01') From sys_setting)
	if DATEDIFF ( month , @lastdate , @currdate ) > 0
	Begin
		insert into ICS_Month_Close(c_id,itm_id,sto_id,ven_id,itm_qty,pharm_price,sell_price,sell_tax,itm_expiry_date,sec_insert_date,itm_purchase_unit,itm_unit1_unit2,itm_unit1_unit3)
		select c_id,ics.itm_id,sto_id,ven_id,itm_qty,pharm_price,itm_def_sell_price sell_price,sell_tax,itm_expiry_date,@currdate sec_insert_date,itm_purchase_unit,itm_unit1_unit2,itm_unit1_unit3
		From item_class_store ics join item_catalog ic on ic.itm_id=ics.itm_id
		Where sto_id in (Select sto_id from store where activated=1)
		and itm_qty<>0
	
		update sys_setting set last_month_close_dt=@currdate
	
	------ ---------- --------------- Undo item change 
	DECLARE @ItemId decimal(18,0) 
	DECLARE @ClassId decimal(18,0) 
	DECLARE @OldQty decimal(18,0) 
	DECLARE @OldPharmPrice money
	DECLARE @OldTaxPrice money

	IF EXISTS (select * from deleted) -- case update
	Begin
		select @ItemId = (SELECT itm_id FROM inserted) 
		select @ClassId = (SELECT c_id FROM inserted) 
		select @OldQty = (SELECT isnull(itm_qty,0) FROM deleted)
		select @OldPharmPrice = (SELECT isnull(pharm_price,0) FROM deleted)
		select @OldTaxPrice = (SELECT isnull(sell_tax,0) FROM deleted)

		update ICS_Month_Close set itm_qty=@OldQty,pharm_price=@OldPharmPrice,sell_tax=@OldTaxPrice
			where itm_id=@ItemId and c_id=@ClassId and sto_id=@StoreId and sec_insert_date=(select last_month_close_dt from sys_setting)
	End
	else--Insert
	Begin
		IF EXISTS (select * from inserted) 
		BEGIN
			select @ItemId = (SELECT itm_id FROM inserted) 
			select @ClassId = (SELECT c_id FROM inserted) 
			delete from ICS_Month_Close where itm_id=@ItemId and c_id=@ClassId and sto_id=@StoreId
		End
	End
	End
End


GO

-- ==========================================
-- TRIGGER: [dbo].[watch_cust_credit_chnge] ON TABLE [Customer]
-- ==========================================
CREATE TRIGGER watch_cust_credit_chnge ON customer FOR INSERT, UPDATE AS DECLARE @custId decimal(18,0) DECLARE @old_credit money DECLARE @new_credit money DECLARE @program_name VARCHAR(200) DECLARE @pc_name VARCHAR(200) IF EXISTS (select * from deleted) BEGIN IF UPDATE(cust_current_credit) BEGIN  select @custId = (SELECT cust_id FROM inserted)  select @old_credit= (SELECT cust_current_credit FROM deleted)  select @new_credit= (SELECT cust_current_credit FROM inserted)  select @program_name= (SELECT program_name from master.dbo.sysprocesses (nolock) where spid=@@spid)  select @pc_name= (SELECT hostname from master.dbo.sysprocesses (nolock) where spid=@@spid)  INSERT INTO customer_credit_chng(cc_cust_id, cc_old_credit, cc_new_credit, cc_prog_name, cc_pc_name, cc_operation) values (@custId, @old_credit, @new_credit, @program_name, @pc_name, 'Update') END END ELSE BEGIN IF EXISTS (select * from inserted) BEGIN  select @custId = (SELECT cust_id FROM inserted)  select @old_credit= (SELECT cust_current_credit FROM deleted)  select @new_credit= (SELECT cust_current_credit FROM inserted)  select @program_name= (SELECT program_name from master.dbo.sysprocesses (nolock) where spid=@@spid)  select @pc_name= (SELECT hostname from master.dbo.sysprocesses (nolock) where spid=@@spid)  INSERT INTO customer_credit_chng(cc_cust_id, cc_old_credit, cc_new_credit, cc_prog_name, cc_pc_name, cc_operation) values (@custId, @old_credit, @new_credit, @program_name, @pc_name, 'Insert') END END

GO

-- ==========================================
-- TRIGGER: [dbo].[watch_vendor_credit_chnge] ON TABLE [Vendor]
-- ==========================================
CREATE TRIGGER watch_vendor_credit_chnge ON vendor FOR INSERT, UPDATE AS DECLARE @vendId decimal(18,0) DECLARE @old_credit money DECLARE @new_credit money DECLARE @program_name VARCHAR(200) DECLARE @pc_name VARCHAR(200) IF EXISTS (select * from deleted) BEGIN IF UPDATE(ven_current_credit) BEGIN  select @vendId = (SELECT ven_id FROM inserted)  select @old_credit= (SELECT ven_current_credit FROM deleted)  select @new_credit= (SELECT ven_current_credit FROM inserted)  select @program_name= (SELECT program_name from master.dbo.sysprocesses (nolock) where spid=@@spid)  select @pc_name= (SELECT hostname from master.dbo.sysprocesses (nolock) where spid=@@spid)  INSERT INTO vendor_credit_chng(vc_cust_id, vc_old_credit, vc_new_credit, vc_prog_name, vc_pc_name, vc_operation) values (@vendId, @old_credit, @new_credit, @program_name, @pc_name, 'Update') END END ELSE BEGIN IF EXISTS (select * from inserted) BEGIN  select @vendId = (SELECT ven_id FROM inserted)  select @old_credit= (SELECT ven_current_credit FROM deleted)  select @new_credit= (SELECT ven_current_credit FROM inserted)  select @program_name= (SELECT program_name from master.dbo.sysprocesses (nolock) where spid=@@spid)  select @pc_name= (SELECT hostname from master.dbo.sysprocesses (nolock) where spid=@@spid)  INSERT INTO vendor_credit_chng(vc_cust_id, vc_old_credit, vc_new_credit, vc_prog_name, vc_pc_name, vc_operation) values (@vendId, @old_credit, @new_credit, @program_name, @pc_name, 'Insert') END END

GO
